Source 7d2133f23e00ad852df8726ead67493e83c901b4 registers the worn Boost option for Crafting cape 9780 and trimmed cape 9781 through the existing equipment component, button decoder and authoritative action path. It validates the current cape in slot 1, membership, base Crafting 99, ordinary item metadata and player protection before applying the shared stat operation to a staged copy. Current 99 becomes 100; repeating at 100 has no effect, and an existing higher boost is preserved. Drained levels gain one point under the existing source arithmetic. XP, inventory, equipment and charges stay unchanged; no RNG or new timer is introduced. Shared stat hooks and outgoing stat notifications are reused.

Correction 129r2 makes guild entry independent from an outfit's member-item flag. The pinned Crafting cape Wiki page explicitly permits F2P guild entry, and both donor entry helpers omit membership checks. All 49 ordinary outfit bindings, visible level requirements, free exits, three-tick door restoration and bank permissions remain unchanged. This entry exception does not grant member cape Boost use. The original 129r1 source and contract remain intact, with the correction linked to its exact SHA.

## FORGE OR-MIG-0131 — Guild cape sales and replacement hoods

Source 35a5f39b72cab3a2655f32ae29bdde54284c9a4b registers Master Crafter 5810 and Head chef 2658 through actual Talk-to and shared NPC dialogue dispatch. A member with base level 99 can buy the matching cape and hood for 99,000 coins. The transaction requires two free slots before payment and stages the strict coin debit and both full item credits together. A failed credit cannot leave a partial reward. The live actor, NPC identity and reach, pending token, membership, level, currency, gear and definitions are rechecked at confirmation. Shared Close and intervening-action cancellation remain in use.

The purchase selects a trimmed cape when another released target skill has base 99. This follows the pinned Wiki seller stock and differs from the donor methods' unconditional untrimmed reward. All 24 target stat records were examined: 23 released skills qualify; unreleased Sailing does not. Existing cape auto-trimming remains separate. Crafting refuses a spare held/worn cape and an already-owned hood; Cooking permits a paid spare cape and preserves its source's additional free hood behavior. Crafting charges at quote acceptance; Cooking charges after the player acknowledgement Continue. A hood is gifted only after both request and NPC agreement pages are continued.
